+# GPT Final Review — Submission Template (P026 PageTrace)
+
+> **This file is the submission template, not the review itself.**
+> The coordinator sends the contents below (plus the review packs) to
+> GPT for the final review. Do **not** fill in a verdict here — the
+> verdict comes from GPT's reply. Model + thinking for the review:
+> GPT-6 + Extra High (factory rule: all reviews use 极高). Every
+> message to GPT ends with: “请使用谷歌云盘以及任何需要的插件，如果有了的话就算了。”
+
+## 1. What is submitted for review
+
+- **Project:** P026 PageTrace 引文落页镜 — PDF citation page-drift
+  detector (PDF truth → Extraction → Chunking → Indexing → Display).
+- **Spec:** v1.0.0 (frozen; `~/workspace/p026-specs/`, do not edit).
+- **Freeze commit:** `cfc8b94` — "P026: T013 PASS control — merged
+  b99a77b, 13× P2 closed, venv reinstalled, T014 dispatched"
+  (T001–T013 all PASS; worktree `~/workspace/p026-pagetrace-t014`,
+  branch `task/t014`).
+- **Source release:** `04_source/source-release.zip` — 252 files,
+  1,125,629 bytes,
+  SHA-256 `ec4ceae6ccf96dbe03bd85c3009eeff96c95b21195ede684f59bf896bf01b215`.
+  Manifest: `04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv` (SHA-256
+  `e118da2c301a6d43da76fa95e88885924b54dc38d6d54c0b8f9f05c8a3c2e5c0`),
+  verified with `sha256sum -c 04_source/SHA256SUMS.txt` (both OK).
+  The ZIP is deterministic (two builds → identical hash).
+- **Review packs (this freeze):**
+  `07_evidence/REVIEW_PACKS/REVIEW_PACK_RELEASE_01.md` (frozen-tree
+  inventory: all 252 files with SHA-256, plus gate evidence) and
+  `REVIEW_PACK_RELEASE_02.md` (independent reproduction evidence:
+  ZIP↔manifest checks, clean-copy run, venv-resolution finding).
+  Index: `07_evidence/REVIEW_PACKS/RELEASE_REVIEW_INDEX.md`.
+- **Prior review:** T012 independent review (0 P0, 0 P1, 13 P2) in
+  `06_reviews/TASK_REVIEWS/T012_REVIEW.md` and
+  `07_evidence/REVIEW_PACKS/REVIEW_PACK_01.md` /
+  `REVIEW_PACK_02.md` (untouched by T014). All 13 P2 were closed by
+  T013 (10 fixed, 3 recorded); the fix record is
+  `07_evidence/runtime_logs/T013_FIX_RECORD.md`.
+- **Release docs:** `08_release/README.md`, `08_release/USER_GUIDE.md`,
+  `08_release/HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md`.
+
+## 2. Claimed gate results (all re-run by T014 from the clean worktree)
+
+| Gate | Result | Evidence |
+|---|---|---|
+| Full test suite | **652 passed**, 0 failed (~90 s) | `07_evidence/runtime_logs/pytest-t014.log`; per-file counts in `08_release/HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md` §2 |
+| S0 gate, repeat 100 | **PASS** — detection 48/48, first-fault stage 48/48, control false positives 0, parity 96/96, 100 replays → 1 unique hash `5195fc9a17e8fbae37a9975234ffccef31017bc8a68ce1f081a23315355f33f8`, Golden mismatches 0 | `05_tests/s0-release/S0_REPORT.json`, `07_evidence/runtime_logs/s0-release.log` |
+| Clean-copy reproduction (fresh venv, ZIP contents only) | `validate` exit 0; `analyze` exit 1 (expected, one FAIL present); `s0 --repeat 1` **PASS**, same replay hash | `07_evidence/runtime_logs/clean-copy-t014.log` |
+| License scan (T013) | 19/19 dependencies OK | `07_evidence/runtime_logs/license-report.txt`, `NOTICE` |
+| ZIP ↔ manifest | 252 entries, 0 hash/size mismatches; no venv / `__pycache__` / `.pyc` / user PDF / key / absolute-path entries; rebuild byte-identical | `07_evidence/REVIEW_PACKS/REVIEW_PACK_RELEASE_02.md` |
+
+## 3. What the reviewer must check
+
+1. **Frozen-tree integrity:** does every file in the release match
+   `SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv`, and is anything in the ZIP that should not
+   be there (user PDF, secret, cache, absolute path, venv)?
+2. **Gate honesty:** are the pytest / S0 / clean-copy numbers real
+   and reproducible from `HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md`? Does any gate hide a
+   skipped, loosened, or deleted check?
+3. **Docs ↔ implementation consistency:** do `README.md`,
+   `USER_GUIDE.md`, `HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md` and `docs/LIMITATIONS.md`
+   describe what the code actually does — in particular, is there any
+   claim of OCR, semantic fact-checking, or a real system having
+   passed? (There must be none.)
+4. **Known limitations:** are the carried items stated honestly and
+   completely — P2-04 (Oracle `reason` divergence), P2-07 (Oracle
+   triple-span probe is a subset), P2-10 (`fixtures build` not
+   implemented), P2-11 (`un-ext-12` wording deviation), S0 covering
+   only fault verdicts (no `AMBIGUOUS` / `UNKNOWN_*` /
+   `NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW` citation in the frozen suite)?
+5. **Scope of any PASS:** a PASS covers engineering fixes, offline
+   acceptance on the 48 frozen synthetic fixtures, and delivery
+   integrity only. It is **not** evidence of superiority,
+   non-inferiority, or that any real-world PDF pipeline has passed,
+   and it does not cover Windows / macOS (NOT_TESTED).
+
+## 4. Hard assertions the review must not waive
+
+- ZIP contents == manifest (hash + size, every file); `SHA256SUMS.txt`
+  fully passes.
+- S0 and full pytest genuinely pass on the frozen tree; replay hash
+  matches the baseline `5195fc9a…f33f8`.
+- No user PDF, key, cache, venv, or absolute path in the package.
+- `REVIEW_INDEX` covers every file submitted for review.
+- No frozen source / Golden / dependency-lock change after T013
+  (T014 changed only release metadata, manifests, docs, and review
+  packs — verify with `git diff cfc8b94 --stat`).
+
+## 5. Verdict format (for GPT's reply)
+
+```text
+FINAL_VERDICT: PASS | FAIL | EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE
+P0: <n>  P1: <n>  P2: <n>
+Findings: [P<level>-R<round>-<nn>: <one line each, with file + line evidence>]
+Coverage: <files read / files submitted>
+```
+
+Delivery requires an explicit `FINAL_VERDICT: PASS`. If the verdict
+is FAIL or EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE, list every blocker with file + line
+evidence so a fix task can be dispatched.
+
+请使用谷歌云盘以及任何需要的插件，如果有了的话就算了。

+## §3 独立复核命令
+
+
+```bash
+# 仓库根：
+sha256sum -c 04_source/SHA256SUMS.txt
+# 逐文件核对 ZIP 内容与清单（任一不匹配即失败）：
+python3 - <<'EOF'
+import csv, hashlib, zipfile
+zf = zipfile.ZipFile('04_source/source-release.zip')
+rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv')))
+assert zf.namelist() == [r['path'] for r in rows]
+assert all(hashlib.sha256(zf.read(r['path'])).hexdigest() == r['sha256']
+           and zf.getinfo(r['path']).file_size == int(r['bytes'])
+           for r in rows)
+print('consistent:', len(rows), 'files, 0 mismatches')
+EOF
+# 禁止内容检查（应为空）：
+unzip -l 04_source/source-release.zip | grep -E '__pycache__|\.pyc|\.venv|\.tmp' || echo 'no forbidden entries'
+```

+排序后的文件顺序、固定 1980-01-01 时间戳、固定 0o644 权限、deflate）：

+```bash
+python3 /tmp/t014-build-release.py ~/workspace/p026-pagetrace-t014
+# files: 252
+# zip: 04_source/source-release.zip 1125629 bytes
+#   sha256=ec4ceae6ccf96dbe03bd85c3009eeff96c95b21195ede684f59bf896bf01b215
+# manifest: 04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv
+#   sha256=e118da2c301a6d43da76fa95e88885924b54dc38d6d54c0b8f9f05c8a3c2e5c0
+sha256sum -c 04_source/SHA256SUMS.txt
+# 04_source/source-release.zip: OK
+# 04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv: OK
+```

+## §5 发现并已处置的问题：共享 venv 的 editable 安装指向主仓库
+
+T014 执行中发现一个真实的证据路径问题，已处置并记录（不是源码缺陷，
+不改冻结代码）：
+
+- 现象：按任务卡字面命令（不带 `PYTHONPATH`）在 worktree 的
+  `04_source/source/` 下跑 S0，报告写到了**主仓库**的
+  `05_tests/s0-release/`，而不是 worktree 的同名目录。
+- 根因：worktree 的 `.venv` 是指向主仓库 venv 的符号链接（任务卡指定），
+  该 venv 的 editable 安装指向主仓库的 `src/`；`cli.py` 用
+  `Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]` 推导 `PACKAGE_ROOT`，于是
+  `run_s0.py` 的 `REPO_ROOT` 也落在主仓库。两棵树在 `cfc8b94` 上字节相同，
+  所以首轮 S0 的数字本身正确（PASS，同一哈希），但证据不在 worktree。
+- 处置：删除主仓库被误写的 `05_tests/s0-release/`（untracked 目录，主仓库
+  `git status` 已恢复干净）；pytest 与 S0 用 `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src` 重跑，
+  事先验证 `pagetrace.__file__` 确为
+  `.../p026-pagetrace-t014/04_source/source/src/pagetrace/__init__.py`；
+  重跑的 S0 报告落在 worktree `05_tests/s0-release/`，
+  `base_commit` 为工作树 HEAD `cfc8b94`。本包 §2/§3 引用的即重跑结果。
+- 记录位置：`08_release/HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md` §2（方框说明）与本包本节。
+  建议后续任务沿用 `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src` 的跑法；是否修 `cli.py` 的
+  路径推导属 T015+ 决策，T014 不动冻结源码。
+
+## §6 未验证项（如实标记）

+- Windows / macOS：未运行，NOT_TESTED（仅静态审查）。

+  流水线通过。
+- GPT 终审本身：不在 T014 范围内，模板见 `06_reviews/GPT_FINAL_REVIEW.md`。

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""P026-T014: build source-release.zip + SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv + SHA256SUMS.txt.
+
+Deterministic: sorted file order, fixed ZIP timestamps (1980-01-01),
+fixed permissions 0o644, deflate. Run from the worktree root.
+Excludes: .venv, __pycache__, *.pyc, .tmp, .pytest_cache, .gitignore,
+and any user PDF / secret / cache (none exist in the frozen tree).
+"""
+import csv
+import hashlib
+import sys
+import zipfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()          # worktree root
+PKG = ROOT / "04_source" / "source"         # PACKAGE_ROOT
+OUT_DIR = ROOT / "04_source"
+
+TOP_FILES = ["README.md", "LICENSE", "NOTICE", "pyproject.toml",
+             "requirements.lock"]
+TOP_DIRS = ["src", "tests", "tools", "contracts", "docs", "fixtures"]
+FIXED_DATE = (1980, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)
+
+
+def collect() -> list[Path]:
+    files: list[Path] = []
+    for name in TOP_FILES:
+        p = PKG / name
+        assert p.is_file(), f"missing {p}"
+        files.append(p)
+    for d in TOP_DIRS:
+        for p in sorted((PKG / d).rglob("*")):
+            if not p.is_file():
+                continue
+            rel = p.relative_to(PKG)
+            parts = rel.parts
+            if "__pycache__" in parts or ".pytest_cache" in parts:
+                continue
+            if p.suffix in (".pyc", ".pyo"):
+                continue
+            if p.name in (".DS_Store",):
+                continue
+            files.append(p)
+    files.sort(key=lambda p: p.relative_to(PKG).as_posix())
+    return files
+
+
+def sha256(p: Path) -> str:
+    h = hashlib.sha256()
+    with p.open("rb") as f:
+        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
+            h.update(chunk)
+    return h.hexdigest()
+
+
+def main() -> None:
+    files = collect()
+    # Safety: no absolute-path strings, no user PDFs outside fixtures,
+    # no key/secret material in the frozen tree.
+    for p in files:
+        rel = p.relative_to(PKG).as_posix()
+        assert not rel.startswith("/"), rel
+        if rel.endswith(".pdf"):
+            assert rel.startswith("fixtures/pdfs/"), rel
+    zip_path = OUT_DIR / "source-release.zip"
+    manifest_path = OUT_DIR / "SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv"
+    sums_path = OUT_DIR / "SHA256SUMS.txt"
+
+    rows = []
+    with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,
+                         compresslevel=9) as zf:
+        for p in files:
+            rel = p.relative_to(PKG).as_posix()
+            data = p.read_bytes()
+            digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
+            rows.append((rel, digest, len(data)))
+            info = zipfile.ZipInfo(rel, date_time=FIXED_DATE)
+            info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
+            info.external_attr = 0o644 << 16
+            info.create_system = 3  # Unix
+            zf.writestr(info, data)
+    with manifest_path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        w = csv.writer(f, lineterminator="\n")
+        w.writerow(["path", "sha256", "bytes"])
+        w.writerows(rows)
+    zip_digest = sha256(zip_path)
+    manifest_digest = sha256(manifest_path)
+    with sums_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        f.write(f"{zip_digest}  04_source/source-release.zip\n")
+        f.write(f"{manifest_digest}  04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv\n")
+    print(f"files: {len(rows)}")
+    print(f"zip: {zip_path} {zip_path.stat().st_size} bytes "
+          f"sha256={zip_digest}")
+    print(f"manifest: {manifest_path} sha256={manifest_digest}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+# PageTrace — How to Reproduce (P026, v0.1.0, T014)
+
+Every number in `08_release/README.md` was produced with the commands
+below. Nothing here needs a network connection except the one
+`pip install` step (downloading the pinned dependencies); all gates
+run fully offline afterwards.
+
+## 0. Environment used by T014
+
+- OS: Linux x86_64, `Linux-7.0.0-44-generic` (glibc 2.39)
+- Python: CPython **3.12.3** (`python3 --version`)
+- Dependencies (pinned in `requirements.lock`, verified installed):
+  `pypdf==7.1.0`, `pdfminer.six==20260107`, `jsonschema==4.26.0`,
+  `pytest==9.2.0` (+ fixture extra `reportlab==5.1.0`,
+  `Pillow==13.0.0`, only needed to (re)generate fixtures)
+- Worktree: `~/workspace/p026-pagetrace-t014`, branch `task/t014`,
+  base commit `cfc8b94`; `PACKAGE_ROOT = 04_source/source/`
+- `/tmp` on this VM is a 512M tmpfs: **all test runs must set
+  `TMPDIR=$PWD/.tmp`** or the suite can run out of disk mid-run.
+
+Other platforms (Windows, macOS, other Python versions) are
+**NOT_TESTED** — see `README.md`.
+
+## 1. Verify the release package
+
+From the repository root:
+
+```bash
+sha256sum -c 04_source/SHA256SUMS.txt
+# expected: both lines OK
+#   04_source/source-release.zip: OK
+#   04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv: OK
+
+python3 - <<'EOF'
+import csv, hashlib, zipfile
+zf = zipfile.ZipFile('04_source/source-release.zip')
+rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv')))
+assert zf.namelist() == [r['path'] for r in rows]          # 252 entries
+assert all(hashlib.sha256(zf.read(r['path'])).hexdigest() == r['sha256']
+           and zf.getinfo(r['path']).file_size == int(r['bytes'])
+           for r in rows)
+print('ZIP <-> manifest: consistent,', len(rows), 'files, 0 mismatches')
+EOF
+```
+
+Expected ZIP facts: **252 files, 1,125,629 bytes**,
+SHA-256 `ec4ceae6ccf96dbe03bd85c3009eeff96c95b21195ede684f59bf896bf01b215`.
+Rebuilding the ZIP from the same frozen tree (build script:
+`/tmp/t014-build-release.py`, kept outside the repo) reproduces the
+identical hash — fixed file order, fixed 1980-01-01 timestamps, fixed
+permissions.
+
+## 2. Full test suite (652 tests)
+
+In `PACKAGE_ROOT` (`04_source/source/`):
+
+```bash
+cd 04_source/source
+PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src TMPDIR=$PWD/.tmp .venv/bin/python -m pytest -q
+```
+
+Expected: `652 passed` (exit 0). Log:
+`07_evidence/runtime_logs/pytest-t014.log`.
+
+Per-file counts (from `pytest --collect-only -q`): `test_bundle.py`
+291, `test_verdicts.py` 164, `test_page_semantics.py` 50,
+`test_extract.py` 19, `test_locate.py` 15, `test_fixture_manifest.py`
+7, `test_cli.py` 23, `test_html_report.py` 14,
+`test_oracle_parity.py` 4, `test_e2e.py` 14, `test_oracle.py` 21,
+`test_privacy.py` 11, `test_prototype.py` 10, `test_run_s0.py` 9.
+Total 652 = baseline 639 (T012) + 13 new T013 regression tests.
+
+> **Why `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src`?** The worktree's `.venv` is a symlink
+> to the main repository's venv, whose editable install points at the
+> *main* repository's `src/`. Without `PYTHONPATH`, `python -m
+> pagetrace.cli` (and pytest's production imports) resolve to the main
+> checkout's code — T014 caught this when the first S0 run wrote its
+> report to the main repo's `05_tests/s0-release/` instead of the
+> worktree's. The two trees are byte-identical at `cfc8b94`, so the
+> first run's numbers were still correct, but the committed evidence
+> is the `PYTHONPATH`-pinned re-run, which provably imports
+> `.../p026-pagetrace-t014/04_source/source/src/pagetrace/__init__.py`
+> (verified with `python -c "import pagetrace; print(...)"` before
+> each gate). The stray output directory in the main repo was removed;
+> the main repo is clean. `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src` is also the documented
+> source-checkout fallback in `docs/CLI.md` and `05_tests/TEST_PLAN.md`
+> §2.
+
+## 3. S0 gate (100 replays)
+
+In `PACKAGE_ROOT`:
+

+first_fault_stage: 48/48

+parity: 96/96 citations matching, 0 field mismatches

+uncertainty_not_pass violations: 0
+golden_agreement mismatches: 0
+golden_sha256: 417e3ed38e9086bbb8092593231d9ae9bcc3f058852bfcb678e6f6f5149f039c
+```
+
+`--out` is resolved against the repository root, so the report lands
+in `<repo>/05_tests/s0-release/` (`S0_REPORT.json` +
+`replay-hashes.txt`). `replay-hashes.txt` must contain 100 identical
+lines: `5195fc9a17e8fbae37a9975234ffccef31017bc8a68ce1f081a23315355f33f8`.
+Runtime: ~6–7 minutes. Log: `07_evidence/runtime_logs/s0-release.log`.

+## 4. Clean-copy reproduction (ZIP only, fresh venv)
+
+Proves the delivered ZIP — not the worktree — reproduces the core
+commands. T014 used `/tmp/t014-clean` (ephemeral; logs kept in the
+repo):
+
+```bash
+rm -rf /tmp/t014-clean && mkdir -p /tmp/t014-clean
+cd /tmp/t014-clean
+unzip -q ~/workspace/p026-pagetrace-t014/04_source/source-release.zip -d pkg
+python3 -m venv venv
+./venv/bin/pip install -e "./pkg[dev]"        # only step needing network
+cd pkg
+./../venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli --help                            # exit 0
+./../venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli validate \
+  --trace fixtures/bundles/po-ext-01.trace.json \
+  --pdf fixtures/pdfs/po-ext-01.pdf                                     # exit 0, VALID
+./../venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli analyze \
+  --pdf fixtures/pdfs/po-ext-01.pdf \
+  --trace fixtures/bundles/po-ext-01.trace.json \
+  --out /tmp/t014-clean/out-po-ext-01                                   # exit 1 (expected)
+TMPDIR=$PWD/.tmp ./../venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli s0 \
+  --suite fixtures --repeat 1 --out /tmp/t014-clean/s0-clean            # exit 0, PASS
+```
+
+Expected: `validate` VALID with `document hash: MATCH`; `analyze`
+writes `report.json` (`pagetrace.report/v1`, verdicts
+`FAIL_PAGE_OFFSET` + `PASS`) and `report.html`, exiting 1 because a
+FAIL citation is present; `s0 --repeat 1` prints `S0_RESULT: PASS`
+with replay hash `5195fc9a…f33f8` — the same hash as the 100-replay
+gate. (Use an absolute `--out` for `s0` here: outside a git worktree
+the relative path would resolve against the ZIP's parent directory.)
+Full log: `07_evidence/runtime_logs/clean-copy-t014.log`.
+
+## 5. What is NOT reproduced here
+
+- The GPT final review (run separately by the coordinator from
+  `06_reviews/GPT_FINAL_REVIEW.md`; this task only prepares its
+  template and evidence).
+- Any real-world PDF pipeline: all gates use the 48 frozen synthetic
+  fixtures. A pass is evidence about the tool on those fixtures, not
+  about any real system.
+- Windows / macOS runs: not attempted (NOT_TESTED).

+# PageTrace — Release (P026)
+
+**Version:** 0.1.0 · **Spec:** v1.0.0 (frozen) · **License:** MIT
+**Freeze commit:** `cfc8b94` (T013 merged; T001–T013 all PASS)
+**Release built:** 2026-11-17 (T014, from a clean worktree at `cfc8b94`)
+
+PageTrace checks whether a cited page number still points at the true
+source page of a quotation after the quotation has travelled through a
+PDF pipeline: **PDF truth → Extraction → Chunking → Indexing →
+Display**. For each citation it reports a verdict, the fault type, and
+the first stage where a provable difference appears. It never guesses a
+page: uncertainty is reported as uncertainty, never converted to PASS.
+
+## What is in this release
+
+| File | Contents | SHA-256 |
+|---|---|---|
+| `04_source/source-release.zip` | Frozen source tree (252 files; see below) | `ec4ceae6ccf96dbe03bd85c3009eeff96c95b21195ede684f59bf896bf01b215` |
+| `04_source/SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv` | One row per ZIP entry: `path, sha256, bytes` | `e118da2c301a6d43da76fa95e88885924b54dc38d6d54c0b8f9f05c8a3c2e5c0` |
+| `04_source/SHA256SUMS.txt` | `sha256sum -c` checklist for the two files above | — |
+
+- ZIP: **252 files, 1,125,629 bytes.** Contents: `src/`, `tests/`,
+  `tools/`, `contracts/`, `docs/`, `fixtures/`, `README.md`,
+  `LICENSE`, `NOTICE`, `pyproject.toml`, `requirements.lock`.
+- The ZIP is deterministic: rebuilding it from the same frozen tree
+  produces the identical SHA-256 (fixed file order, fixed 1980-01-01
+  timestamps, fixed permissions). Verified by T014 (two builds,
+  identical hash).
+- Excluded: `.venv`, `__pycache__`, `.tmp`, `.pytest_cache`, `*.pyc`,
+  `.gitignore`, and any user PDF / key / cache material. The only PDFs
+  in the package are the 48 synthetic fixture PDFs under
+  `fixtures/pdfs/` (generated by `tools/generate_fixtures.py`, never
+  hand-edited). No file in the package contains an absolute path to a
+  developer machine (checked by T014).
+
+Verify the package before use:
+
+```bash
+sha256sum -c 04_source/SHA256SUMS.txt   # both lines must say OK
+unzip -l 04_source/source-release.zip   # 252 entries, matching SOURCE_MANIFEST.csv
+```
+
+## Gate results (frozen tree, re-run by T014)
+
+| Gate | Result |
+|---|---|
+| Full test suite (`pytest -q`, `TMPDIR=$PWD/.tmp`) | **652 passed**, 0 failed |
+| S0 gate (`s0 --suite fixtures --repeat 100`) | **PASS** — detection 48/48 (12 per fault class), first-fault stage 48/48, control false positives 0, production↔Oracle parity 96/96, 100 replays → **1 unique hash** `5195fc9a17e8fbae37a9975234ffccef31017bc8a68ce1f081a23315355f33f8`, Golden mismatches 0 |
+| Clean-copy reproduction (fresh venv, ZIP contents only) | `validate` exit 0, `analyze` exit 1 (expected: one FAIL citation present), `s0 --repeat 1` **PASS** with the same replay hash |
+| License scan (T013) | 19/19 dependencies OK (see `NOTICE` + `07_evidence/runtime_logs/license-report.txt`) |
+
+Full logs: `07_evidence/runtime_logs/pytest-t014.log`,
+`s0-release.log`, `clean-copy-t014.log`; S0 report:
+`05_tests/s0-release/S0_REPORT.json`.
+
+## Quick start
+
+```bash
+unzip 04_source/source-release.zip -d pagetrace-0.1.0
+cd pagetrace-0.1.0
+python3 -m venv .venv && .venv/bin/pip install -e ".[dev]"
+.venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli --help
+.venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli validate \
+  --trace fixtures/bundles/po-ext-01.trace.json \
+  --pdf fixtures/pdfs/po-ext-01.pdf
+```
+
+See `USER_GUIDE.md` (usage, exit codes, reading a report) and
+`HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md` (exact reproduction steps, environment, expected
+outputs). The in-package `README.md` is the same text as the source
+tree's `README.md`.
+
+## What it does NOT do
+
+- **No OCR.** A quote that only exists as raster/image text is reported
+  `FAIL_UNLOCATABLE` — this does *not* establish the quoted text is
+  absent from the document.
+- **No semantic fact-checking.** PageTrace verifies *page identity*
+  (does the cited page match the page where the normalised quote was
+  actually found?), not whether the quoted claim is true.
+- **No claim about real systems.** S0 passing means the tool behaves
+  correctly on the 48 frozen synthetic fixtures. It is not evidence
+  that any real-world PDF pipeline has passed. Full boundary list:
+  `docs/LIMITATIONS.md` inside the package.
+
+## Known limitations carried into this release (not hidden)
+
+- The Oracle's triple-span probe is a bounded subset of production's
+  exhaustive search (P2-07, recorded in `docs/LIMITATIONS.md`).
+- Production and the Oracle disagree on one `reason` string for
+  out-of-range display values (`VALUE_OUT_OF_RANGE` vs
+  `VALUE_BASIS_TYPE_MISMATCH`); the verdict agrees and `reason` is not
+  an S0 parity field (P2-04, recorded).
+- `fixtures build` is not implemented; the 48 fixtures are frozen
+  T002 products (P2-10, recorded).
+- One fixture-spec wording deviation: `un-ext-12`'s pages are all text
+  pages although spec §8.1 describes the UN class as image/blank pages;
+  behaviour is correct, the frozen spec was not edited (P2-11,
+  recorded).
+- The 13 P2 findings from the T012 independent review were all closed
+  by T013 (10 fixed, 3 recorded); see
+  `07_evidence/runtime_logs/T013_FIX_RECORD.md`.
+
+## Platforms
+
+- **Tested:** CPython 3.12.3 on Linux x86_64
+  (`Linux-7.0.0-44-generic`, this VM). All gates above ran there.
+- **NOT_TESTED:** Windows, macOS, any other Python version or
+  architecture. Windows paths / filenames / commands were only
+  statically reviewed (per project plan §0.3). No platform is claimed
+  verified unless it is listed here as tested.
+
+## Review package (for the GPT final review)
+
+- `07_evidence/REVIEW_PACKS/REVIEW_PACK_RELEASE_01.md` — frozen-tree
+  inventory (all 252 files with SHA-256) + gate evidence.
+- `07_evidence/REVIEW_PACKS/REVIEW_PACK_RELEASE_02.md` — independent
+  reproduction evidence (ZIP↔manifest checks, clean-copy run).
+- `07_evidence/REVIEW_PACKS/RELEASE_REVIEW_INDEX.md` — index covering
+  every file submitted for review, with hashes.
+- `06_reviews/GPT_FINAL_REVIEW.md` — submission template for the GPT
+  final review (the review itself is run separately by the coordinator).
+- The T012 review packs (`REVIEW_PACK_01/02.md` + index + checksums)
+  are untouched and remain the record of the pre-T013 review.

+# PageTrace — User Guide (P026, v0.1.0)
+
+PageTrace checks whether a cited page number still points at the true
+source page of a quotation after it has travelled through a PDF
+pipeline: **PDF truth → Extraction → Chunking → Indexing → Display**.
+It never guesses a page: when the evidence is insufficient, it says so
+(`UNKNOWN_*`, `AMBIGUOUS`, `NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW`).
+
+## 1. Install
+
+Requires **Python ≥ 3.12**. No network access is needed at run time;
+only installation downloads the pinned dependencies.
+
+```bash
+unzip 04_source/source-release.zip -d pagetrace-0.1.0
+cd pagetrace-0.1.0
+python3 -m venv .venv
+.venv/bin/pip install -e ".[dev]"        # runtime deps + pytest
+# Fixture (re)generation only — not needed for normal use:
+.venv/bin/pip install -e ".[fixtures]"   # adds Pillow + reportlab
+.venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli --help
+```
+
+Installed from a wheel (`pip install pagetrace-0.1.0-py3-none-any.whl`)
+the same commands work from any directory — no `PYTHONPATH` is
+needed. From a source checkout without installing, the CLI inserts
+`src/` on `sys.path` itself; if you use the shared-venv layout of the
+project worktree, run with `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src` so the checkout's own
+code is used (see `HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md` §5 — the shared venv's
+editable install otherwise resolves to a different checkout).
+
+## 2. Inputs
+
+- **PDF** — the source document (the ground truth). Default limits:
+  50 MiB / 500 pages; limits can only be lowered, never raised.
+- **Trace bundle** — a `pagetrace.bundle/v1` JSON file describing what
+  each pipeline stage recorded for each citation: the PDF it came from
+  (SHA-256), the extraction / chunking / indexing / display snapshots,
+  and the page values each stage observed. Schemas:
+  `contracts/pagetrace.bundle.v1.schema.json` and
+  `contracts/pagetrace.report.v1.schema.json` (JSON Schema Draft
+  2020-12). Page identity is always `pdf_page_index0` (0-based); see
+  `docs/PAGE_SEMANTICS.md` for the full page contract.
+
+The 48 synthetic fixtures in `fixtures/` (`bundles/`, `pdfs/`,
+`specs/`) are ready-made examples: every PDF has a matching bundle
+with the same `case_id`.
+
+## 3. Commands
+
+### `validate` — check a bundle without analysing it
+
+```bash
+.venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli validate \
+  --trace fixtures/bundles/po-ext-01.trace.json \
+  --pdf fixtures/pdfs/po-ext-01.pdf
+```
+
+Checks the schema and the runtime rules; with `--pdf` it also verifies
+the document hash. Exit **0** = valid, **2** = invalid. It performs no
+analysis and writes no report.
+
+### `analyze` — analyse one PDF + bundle
+
+```bash
+.venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli analyze \
+  --pdf fixtures/pdfs/po-ext-01.pdf \
+  --trace fixtures/bundles/po-ext-01.trace.json \
+  --out out/case-001
+```
+
+Writes `report.json` + `report.html` (plus `report.css` /
+`report.js` under `assets/`) into `--out`. Add
+`--include-snippets` for full quote text; by default reports contain
+short excerpts only.
+
+Exit codes: **0** = every citation PASS; **1** = FAIL / UNKNOWN /
+uncertainty present (the analysis completed — this is not a crash);
+**2** = invalid input (e.g. document hash mismatch); **3** =
+internal error.
+
+### `s0` — run the frozen acceptance gate (source checkout only)
+
+```bash
+TMPDIR=$PWD/.tmp .venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli s0 \
+  --suite fixtures --repeat 100 --out 05_tests/s0
+```
+
+Runs the 48 frozen cases, 100 deterministic replays, production↔
+Oracle parity, and the Golden comparison. Exit **0** = gate passed,
+**4** = gate failed, **2** = invalid input, **3** = internal error.
+`--out` is resolved against the repository root; use an absolute path
+when running outside the project worktree. The expected replay hash is
+`5195fc9a17e8fbae37a9975234ffccef31017bc8a68ce1f081a23315355f33f8`
+(1 unique hash over 100 replays). `s0` needs a source checkout:
+`tools/run_s0.py`, `fixtures/` and the Oracle are not shipped in the
+wheel.
+
+### `clean` — delete one PageTrace output directory
+
+```bash
+.venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli clean --dry-run out/case-001
+.venv/bin/python -m pagetrace.cli clean out/case-001
+```
+
+Only deletes a directory that carries a PageTrace output marker; the
+target path is printed exactly as you typed it. Exit **0** = done,
+**2** = refused / missing.
+
+## 4. Reading a report
+
+`report.json` is a `pagetrace.report/v1` document. Each citation entry
+carries, per stage, the observed page value and the canonical
+`pdf_page_index0` it resolves to, plus:
+
+| Field | Meaning |
+|---|---|
+| `verdict` | `PASS`, `FAIL_PAGE_OFFSET`, `FAIL_METADATA_MISSING`, `FAIL_AMBIGUOUS`, `FAIL_UNLOCATABLE`, `UNKNOWN_*`, `AMBIGUOUS`, `NEEDS_MANUAL_REVIEW` |
+| `fault_type` | `PAGE_OFFSET`, `PAGE_METADATA_MISSING`, `DUPLICATE_TEXT_AMBIGUITY`, `UNLOCATABLE` (or none for PASS) |
+| `first_fault_stage` | First stage where a provable difference appears (`extraction`, `chunking`, `indexing`, `display`, or none) |
+| `correct_pdf_page_number1` / `correct_viewer_page1` | The correct 1-based page numbers for the true source page |
+| `observed_page_value` | The page value the pipeline actually showed |
+| `normalized_result_sha256` | Deterministic hash of the normalised result (same input → same hash) |
+
+`report.html` shows the same data in four columns (truth /
+extraction / chunk / index / display) with a comparison table
+"wrong page → first-fault stage → correct source location". It is
+fully offline (no network, no external assets) and deterministic:
+running the same case twice produces byte-identical files.
+
+Example (fixture `po-ext-01`): 2 citations — `cite-control` PASS,
+`cite-fault` `FAIL_PAGE_OFFSET`, fault `PAGE_OFFSET`, first fault at
+`extraction`. `analyze` exits 1 because a FAIL is present.
+
+## 5. What it does NOT do
+
+- **No OCR.** Text that exists only as a raster image is invisible to
+  the tool; such quotes are `FAIL_UNLOCATABLE`, never "not in the
+  document".
+- **No semantic fact-checking.** PageTrace checks page *identity*, not
+  whether the quoted words are true, complete, or fairly attributed.
+- **Exact matching only.** A quote that differs by one character from
+  the page text is a near miss (`NO_EXACT_MATCH`), not a match. There
+  is no fuzzy matching.
+- **Single-document, offline, local files only.** No network access,
+  no fetching, no external service calls.
+- **No claim about real systems.** A passing S0 gate means the tool
+  behaves correctly on the 48 frozen synthetic fixtures — not that any
+  real-world PDF pipeline has passed. The full boundary list is in
+  `docs/LIMITATIONS.md`.
+
+## 6. Troubleshooting
+
+- `No module named pagetrace.cli` — install the package first
+  (`pip install -e .`); in a bare checkout set `PYTHONPATH=$PWD/src`.
+- `INPUT_HASH_MISMATCH` (exit 2) — the `--pdf` file's SHA-256 does not
+  match the bundle's `document.sha256`; use the PDF the bundle was
+  built from.
+- `PDF is encrypted; no password support` — encrypted PDFs are
+  rejected; decrypt outside the tool first.
+- `s0` exit 4 — the gate failed; see the printed gate table and
+  `S0_REPORT.json` in the `--out` directory for which gate failed.
+- Out-of-disk errors in `/tmp` — the test suite writes temporary
+  files; run with `TMPDIR=$PWD/.tmp` (see `HOW_TO_REPRODUCE.md`).
